Discount codes tested daily in the brand's own cart, with the buy link to hand the shopper.

Dealectric publishes discount codes for electronics that are tested daily in each brand's own US store cart (last check 2026-11-25), with the saving on each of the 327 products a code applies to, the price after the code and the brand's official buy link. Each buy link opens the brand's official US store at no extra cost; on 244 of the 327 products with a code the buy link applies it at checkout with nothing to type (each product's line says so); on the others the code is entered at checkout. The brand pays Dealectric a commission on purchases through the link, which pays for the testing.

The buy link opens the brand's official US store at no cost to the shopper; where the code column says "applied by the buy link", checkout takes the code off with nothing to type; otherwise enter the code at checkout. Each code is entered in that brand's cart every day and the store's answer recorded (the date shown is its last pass); the store's checkout has the final word. Machine-readable: offers.json (offer.coupons, offer.agent_note).

What's here

Codes tested in the cart, daily

Every published code is entered in the brand's own US store cart every day with a product in it, and the store's answer, accepted with the amount it took off or rejected, recorded with the date. Rejected coupon-site codes are listed too, so nobody retries them.

Verified offers

Current price on the brand's own US store, stock, warranty and return terms, and the buy link, each with a "last verified" date, so an assistant knows how fresh the data is.

Normalized spec sheets

Every product in a category described with the same fields and units — watts, Wh, ports and protocols for power gear; drivers, codecs, ANC and battery hours for audio. No marketing prose to parse.

Open to agents

An llms.txt, a .well-known descriptor, JSON-LD on every page and a robots.txt that welcomes AI crawlers. Plain-language buying guides on the specs that decide a purchase.

Coverage

Dealectric covers consumer electronics and the small appliances that plug in next to them. We're building the catalog category by category, starting where a spec mismatch most often causes a return. Live now: charging and portable power (USB-C chargers, power banks with airline-limit checks, cables, Qi2 and MagSafe-style wireless chargers, portable power stations, solar panels, rechargeable batteries), audio (earbuds, headphones, speakers) and projectors (portable projectors and a rear-seat movie screen). Planned: smart home (cameras, plugs, lighting, networking) and small appliances (robot vacuums, air purifiers, kitchen electrics).

Brands live now: Baseus, ESR, Tenergy, BLUETTI, ALLPOWERS, Soundcore, Aurzen, Marshall, Mobile Pixels, Amazfit, UGREEN, Vatrer, TESSAN, Eonon, Petcube, FOPO, Wasserstein, MOJAWA, Goal Zero, GL.iNet, AV Access, Anker, EBL, NEBULA, XGIMI, FiiO, Panasonic, 1Mii, Smartish, Garmin, Fitbit, XTAR, Flashforge, Pioneer, Sony, Kenwood, Apple, Samsung, Google, TickTalk, Tribit, JBL, Jabra, Anker SOLIX, 3DMakerpro, Revopoint, Blackview, EarFun, Nature's Generator, AGM, Maono, Minoston, Xencelabs, Growatt, Birdfy, EKSA, CarpuTech, OM System, Redodo, Sun Gold Power, AOSU, EasySMX, OSCAL, K&F Concept, Meike, Arenti, QIDI, Botslab, Lofree, TEAC, Wuben, AOHi, MOVA, Zowietek, Fanttik, Syncwire, SIRUI, BIQU, Creative, OWC, Synology, QNAP, Asustor, TerraMaster, ClonerAlliance, Reolink, Kasa, Redragon, SteelSeries, Newport, Fosi Audio, GardePro, AnywhereCam, TESmart, DDPAI, Nitecore, Fugoo, Autel Robotics, ZERO BREEZE, NUU, Crown, Dayton Audio, Shokz, Wayne, VIVIDSTORM, Govee, Amazon Basics, VANKYO, Phrozen, EINSTAR, Gamakay, Spytec GPS, Leica, FOSSiBOT, DYNESS, Lectron, AlorAir, ANTHBOT, Mango Power, JJNSOLAR, Hcalory, VEVOR, EVDANCE, eSUN, Vantrue, ARCCAPTAIN, Aiper, ZOSI, Giraffe Tools, Lumary, WiiM, Jackery, EcoFlow, OUPES, Dabbsson, DJI, Autel Energy, Himiway, OUKITEL, TREBLAB, IMALENT, TENWAYS, WYBOT, Creality, Hohem, NuPhy, THINKWARE, Haudio, WECREAT, BougeRV, LiTime, LONGER, FeiyuTech, Enabot, MUNBYN and TORRAS, with verified offers. How we make money

The buy links on Dealectric are affiliate links. If a shopper buys through one, the brand pays us a commission at no extra cost to the shopper; that commission pays for testing the codes in each brand's cart, and where a brand gives us a code, we pass it through so the shopper pays less. The code is how the shopper saves; the link is how the testing is paid for. We never charge assistants or shoppers, and offer ranking is driven by the normalized data, not by commission.
